Ma<sub>p</sub>An<sub>y</sub>thin<sub>g</sub> [51] factorizes the reconstruction interface into ra<sub>y</sub>s, <sup>d</sup>e<sub>p</sub>t<sup>h</sup>, <sub>p</sub>ose, an<sup>d</sup> sca<sup>l</sup>e, a <sub>p</sub>ers<sub>p</sub>ect<sup>i</sup>ve our as<sub>y</sub>mmetr<sup>i</sup>c <sub>source</sub>/t<sub>arge</sub>t <sub>pac</sub>k<sub>e</sub>t d<sub>es</sub>i<sub>gn s</sub>h<sub>ares.</sub> O<sub>n</sub> th<sub>e</sub> 3D <sub>s</sub>id<sub>e, se</sub>lf<sub>-</sub> su<sub>p</sub>ervised <sub>p</sub>oint transformers [52], [53] have culminated in Utonia [24], a sin<sub>g</sub>le <sub>p</sub>oint-cloud encoder across domains <sub>w</sub>h<sub>ose</sub> f<sub>ea</sub>t<sub>ures</sub> h<sub>ave a</sub>l<sub>rea</sub>d<sub>y proven use</sub>f<sub>u</sub>l f<sub>or em</sub>b<sub>o</sub>di<sub>e</sub>d <sub>rea-</sub> <sub>son</sub>i<sub>ng.</sub> Th<sub>ese</sub> 2D <sub>an</sub>d 3D <sub>mo</sub>d<sub>e</sub>l<sub>s o</sub>f<sub>er comp</sub>l<sub>emen</sub>t<sub>ary pr</sub>i<sub>ors</sub> l<sub>earne</sub>d f<sub>rom</sub> f<sub>ar</sub> b<sub>roa</sub>d<sub>er</sub> d<sub>a</sub>t<sub>a</sub> th<sub>an a s</sub>i<sub>ng</sub>l<sub>e ro</sub>b<sub>o</sub>t <sub>co</sub>ll<sub>ec</sub>ti<sub>on.</sub>

## B. Difusion and Flow Matching

A dif<sub>us</sub>i<sub>on</sub> <sub>mo</sub>d<sub>e</sub>l i<sub>s</sub> <sub>a</sub> <sub>con</sub>diti<sub>ona</sub>l <sub>genera</sub>t<sub>or</sub> th<sub>a</sub>t l<sub>earns</sub> t<sub>o</sub> recover a clean sam<sub>p</sub>le from a deliberatel<sub>y</sub> corru<sub>p</sub>ted one [62]: <sub>a</sub> <sub>ne</sub>t<sub>wor</sub>k i<sub>s</sub> t<sub>ra</sub>i<sub>ne</sub>d t<sub>o</sub> <sub>pre</sub>di<sub>c</sub>t <sub>a</sub> <sub>quan</sub>tit<sub>y</sub> <sub>po</sub>i<sub>n</sub>ti<sub>ng</sub> b<sub>ac</sub>k t<sub>owar</sub>d the clean data distribution, such as the injected noise, the clean <sub>samp</sub>l<sub>e, or an equ</sub>i<sub>va</sub>l<sub>en</sub>t <sub>ve</sub>l<sub>oc</sub>it<sub>y.</sub> L<sub>e</sub>t $z _ { \mathrm { 0 } }$ d<sub>eno</sub>t<sub>e</sub> th<sub>e repre-</sub> sentation to be generated and y the conditioning information. In <sub>p</sub>i<sub>xe</sub>l<sub>-space</sub> im<sub>age ge</sub>n<sub>e</sub>r<sub>a</sub>ti<sub>o</sub>n<sub>,</sub> $z _ { \mathrm { 0 } }$ i<sub>s o</sub>ft<sub>en</sub> th<sub>e</sub> RGB i<sub>mage</sub> it<sub>se</sub>lf<sub>.</sub> I<sub>n</sub> thi<sub>s paper, genera</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> i<sub>ns</sub>t<sub>ea</sub>d <sub>opera</sub>t<sub>es</sub> i<sub>n a</sub> l<sub>a</sub>t<sub>en</sub>t s<sub>p</sub>ace, w<sup>h</sup>ere $z _ { \mathrm { 0 } }$ i<sub>s a mu</sub>lti<sub>-v</sub>i<sub>ew</sub> f<sub>ea</sub>t<sub>ure</sub> t<sub>ensor</sub> l<sub>a</sub>t<sub>er</sub> d<sub>eco</sub>d<sub>e</sub>d i<sub>n</sub>t<sub>o</sub> RGB <sub>an</sub>d d<sub>ep</sub>th<sub>.</sub> O<sub>ur</sub> b<sub>ac</sub>kb<sub>one</sub> i<sub>ns</sub>t<sub>an</sub>ti<sub>a</sub>t<sub>es</sub> thi<sub>s</sub> <sub>genera</sub>t<sub>or</sub> with flow matchin<sub>g</sub>, a continuous-time objective [29], [30] that <sub>rep</sub>l<sub>aces</sub> th<sub>e</sub> di<sub>scre</sub>t<sub>e</sub> d<sub>eno</sub>i<sub>s</sub>i<sub>ng</sub> <sub>c</sub>h<sub>a</sub>i<sub>n</sub> <sub>w</sub>ith <sub>a</sub> <sub>pa</sub>th b<sub>e</sub>t<sub>ween</sub> th<sub>e</sub> <sub>c</sub>l<sub>ean</sub> l<sub>a</sub>t<sub>en</sub>t $z _ { 0 }$ <sub>an</sub>d <sub>a pure no</sub>i<sub>se samp</sub>l<sub>e</sub> $z _ { 1 } \sim \mathcal { N } ( 0 , \mathbf { I } )$ , where I i<sub>s</sub> th<sub>e</sub> id<sub>en</sub>tit<sub>y ma</sub>t<sub>r</sub>i<sub>x over</sub> th<sub>e</sub> fl<sub>a</sub>tt<sub>ene</sub>d l<sub>a</sub>t<sub>en</sub>t di<sub>mens</sub>i<sub>on.</sub> F<sub>or</sub> the linear <sub>p</sub>ath used in GLD [8]

$$
z _ { t } = ( 1 - t ) z _ { 0 } + t z _ { 1 } , \qquad t \in [ 0 , 1 ] .\tag{1}
$$

Th<sub>e</sub> id<sub>ea</sub>l <sub>ve</sub>l<sub>oc</sub>it<sub>y</sub> i<sub>s</sub> <sub>s</sub>i<sub>mp</sub>l<sub>y</sub> th<sub>e</sub> di<sub>rec</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> f<sub>rom</sub> d<sub>a</sub>t<sub>a</sub> t<sub>o</sub> <sub>no</sub>i<sub>se</sub>

$$
u _ { t } = \frac { \mathrm { d } z _ { t } } { \mathrm { d } t } = z _ { 1 } - z _ { 0 } .\tag{2}
$$

A <sub>ne</sub>t<sub>wor</sub>k $v _ { \theta } ( z _ { t } , t , y )$ with parameters θ is trained to predict thi<sub>s</sub> <sub>ve</sub>l<sub>oc</sub>it<sub>y</sub> <sub>un</sub>d<sub>er</sub> th<sub>e</sub> <sub>con</sub>diti<sub>ona</sub>l l<sub>oss</sub>

$$
\begin{array} { r } { \mathcal { L } _ { \mathrm { F M } } = \mathbb { E } _ { z _ { 0 } , z _ { 1 } , t } \left[ \left\| v _ { \theta } ( z _ { t } , t , y ) - u _ { t } \right\| _ { 2 } ^ { 2 } \right] . } \end{array}\tag{3}
$$

Fi<sub>g</sub>. 2. Overview of M3GD. (a) Posed source views <sub>p</sub>rovide RGB, LiDAR, and sensor <sub>p</sub>oses, while the tar<sub>g</sub>et quer<sub>y</sub> su<sub>pp</sub>lies onl<sub>y</sub> a camera <sub>p</sub>ose and LiDAR; tar<sub>g</sub>et RGB is never observed. (b) The frozen DA3 encoder ma<sub>p</sub>s source ima<sub>g</sub>es to ima<sub>g</sub>e latents. From the same LiDAR swee<sub>p</sub>, camera <sub>p</sub>rojection constructs <sub>exp</sub>li<sub>c</sub>it <sub>geome</sub>t<sub>ry s</sub>t<sub>a</sub>ti<sub>s</sub>ti<sub>cs, w</sub>hil<sub>e</sub> th<sub>e</sub> f<sub>rozen</sub> Ut<sub>on</sub>i<sub>a enco</sub>d<sub>er supp</sub>li<sub>es</sub> l<sub>earne</sub>d <sub>per-po</sub>i<sub>n</sub>t d<sub>escr</sub>i<sub>p</sub>t<sub>ors</sub> th<sub>a</sub>t <sub>are poo</sub>l<sub>e</sub>d i<sub>n</sub>t<sub>o</sub> th<sub>e correspon</sub>di<sub>ng</sub> i<sub>mage-</sub>l<sub>a</sub>t<sub>en</sub>t <sub>ce</sub>ll<sub>s;</sub> their concatenation forms the per-view LiDAR packet. Fig. 1 details this projected RGB–LiDAR representation. A zero-initialized residual adapter composes this <sub>p</sub>acket with the DA3 <sub>g</sub>rid cell b<sub>y</sub> cell. (c) The resultin<sub>g</sub> source tokens (RGB + LiDAR) and tar<sub>g</sub>et tokens (noise + LiDAR quer<sub>y</sub>) enter the multi-view flow matching network, which integrates the learned velocit<sub>y</sub> field for K Euler steps to produce the target latent; frozen decoders recover RGB and depth Arrow colors distin<sub>g</sub>uish the ima<sub>g</sub>e-a<sub>pp</sub>earance (blue), metric-<sub>g</sub>eometr<sub>y</sub> (teal), and <sub>g</sub>eneration (<sub>p</sub>ur<sub>p</sub>le) <sub>p</sub>athwa<sub>y</sub>s.

<sub>an</sub>d <sub>con</sub>diti<sub>on</sub>i<sub>ng mec</sub>h<sub>an</sub>i<sub>sm</sub> b<sub>ecause</sub> M3GD <sub>re</sub>t<sub>a</sub>i<sub>ns</sub> GLD’<sub>s</sub> <sub>genera</sub>ti<sub>ve</sub> <sub>p</sub>i<sub>pe</sub>li<sub>ne</sub> <sub>an</sub>d <sub>mo</sub>difi<sub>es</sub> it<sub>s</sub> <sub>per-v</sub>i<sub>ew</sub> <sub>con</sub>diti<sub>on</sub>i<sub>ng</sub> f<sub>ea</sub>t<sub>u</sub>r<sub>e</sub> in S<sub>ec</sub>ti<sub>o</sub>n IV<sub>-</sub>D<sub>.</sub>

Latent re<sub>p</sub>resentation and reado<sub>u</sub>t<sub>.</sub> GLD fir<sub>s</sub>t fi<sub>xes</sub> th<sub>e</sub> <sub>space</sub> i<sub>n</sub> <sub>w</sub>hi<sub>c</sub>h <sub>genera</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> <sub>occurs</sub> <sub>us</sub>i<sub>ng</sub> <sub>one</sub> f<sub>rozen</sub> <sub>enco</sub>d<sub>er</sub> <sub>an</sub>d t<sub>wo</sub> f<sub>rozen</sub> d<sub>eco</sub>d<sub>ers</sub>

$$
\begin{array} { r l } & { z = \mathrm { E } _ { \mathrm { D A 3 } } ( I ) \in \mathbb { R } ^ { C \times H \times W } , } \\ & { \hat { I } = \mathrm { D } _ { \mathrm { R G B } } ( z ) , \qquad \hat { D } = \mathrm { D P T } ( z ) , } \end{array}\tag{4}
$$

where z is a latent grid of $H \times W$ <sub>ce</sub>ll<sub>s</sub> <sub>a</sub>li<sub>gne</sub>d <sub>w</sub>ith th<sub>e</sub> i<sub>mage</sub> patch grid and carr<sub>y</sub>ing C channels respectivel<sub>y</sub>, D<sub>RGB</sub> is a frozen MAE decoder, DPT is a frozen depth decoder [63], and ˆ· denotes a model <sub>p</sub>rediction throu<sub>g</sub>hout.

The DA3 encoder in eq. (4) ex<sub>p</sub>oses a hierarch<sub>y</sub> of four feature levels, indexed 0–3 from shallowest to deepest. Because th<sub>e</sub> f<sub>rozen</sub> <sub>enco</sub>d<sub>er</sub> <sub>can</sub> d<sub>e</sub>t<sub>erm</sub>i<sub>n</sub>i<sub>s</sub>ti<sub>ca</sub>ll<sub>y</sub> <sub>propaga</sub>t<sub>e</sub> <sub>a</sub> <sub>s</sub>h<sub>a</sub>ll<sub>ower</sub> f<sub>ea</sub>t<sub>ure</sub> <sub>map</sub> t<sub>o</sub> d<sub>eeper</sub> l<sub>eve</sub>l<sub>s,</sub> <sub>genera</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> <sub>nee</sub>d<sub>s</sub> <sub>on</sub>l<sub>y</sub> t<sub>o</sub> <sub>cover</sub> th<sub>e</sub> hi<sub>erarc</sub>h<sub>y</sub> <sub>up</sub> t<sub>o</sub> <sub>a</sub> <sub>c</sub>h<sub>osen</sub> b<sub>oun</sub>d<sub>ary</sub> l<sub>eve</sub>l<sub>.</sub> GLD <sub>repor</sub>t<sub>s</sub> it<sub>s</sub> b<sub>es</sub>t qualit<sub>y</sub> b<sub>y</sub> explicitl<sub>y</sub> s<sub>y</sub>nthesizing levels 0 and 1 in a cascade: a level-1 model first generates the deeper latent, and a level-0 <sub>mo</sub>d<sub>e</sub>l th<sub>en genera</sub>t<sub>es</sub> th<sub>e s</sub>h<sub>a</sub>ll<sub>ower</sub> l<sub>a</sub>t<sub>en</sub>t <sub>con</sub>diti<sub>one</sub>d <sub>on</sub> th<sub>e</sub> level-1 prediction. M3GD instead adopts a single-model oper-<sub>a</sub>ti<sub>ng po</sub>i<sub>n</sub>t <sub>su</sub>it<sub>e</sub>d t<sub>o on</sub>b<sub>oar</sub>d <sub>ro</sub>b<sub>o</sub>t d<sub>ep</sub>l<sub>oymen</sub>t <sub>an</sub>d <sub>syn</sub>th<sub>es</sub>i<sub>zes</sub> onl<sub>y</sub> level 0. ## C. Projected RGB–LiDAR Representation

The projected RGB–LiDAR representation provides the con-<sub>nec</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> b<sub>e</sub>t<sub>ween</sub> th<sub>e</sub> LiDAR <sub>o</sub>b<sub>serva</sub>ti<sub>on an</sub>d th<sub>e</sub> i<sub>mage-</sub>l<sub>a</sub>t<sub>en</sub>t NVS b<sub>ac</sub>kb<sub>one.</sub> F<sub>or eac</sub>h <sub>source or</sub> t<sub>arge</sub>t <sub>v</sub>i<sub>ew, a ca</sub>lib<sub>ra</sub>t<sub>e</sub>d LiDAR <sub>sweep</sub> f<sub>o</sub>ll<sub>ows</sub> t<sub>wo para</sub>ll<sub>e</sub>l <sub>comp</sub>l<sub>emen</sub>t<sub>ary pa</sub>th<sub>s:</sub> <sub>an exp</sub>li<sub>c</sub>it <sub>pa</sub>th th<sub>a</sub>t <sub>summar</sub>i<sub>zes</sub> th<sub>e me</sub>t<sub>r</sub>i<sub>c measuremen</sub>t<sub>s</sub> <sub>suppor</sub>ti<sub>ng</sub> <sub>eac</sub>h i<sub>mage-</sub>l<sub>a</sub>t<sub>en</sub>t <sub>ce</sub>ll<sub>,</sub> <sub>an</sub>d <sub>a</sub> l<sub>earne</sub>d <sub>pa</sub>th th<sub>a</sub>t <sub>poo</sub>l<sub>s</sub> frozen Utonia [24] <sub>p</sub>er-<sub>p</sub>oint descri<sub>p</sub>tors into the same cells.

Sweep selection and projection. For a view with image t<sup>i</sup>mestam<sub>p</sub> $\tau ,$ <sub>we se</sub>l<sub>ec</sub>t th<sub>e</sub> LiDAR <sub>sweep</sub> $P = \left\{ p _ { k } \right\}$ <sub>w</sub>h<sub>ose</sub> timestamp τ<sub>P</sub> is nearest τ , which minimizes camera–LiDAR <sub>m</sub>i<sub>sma</sub>t<sub>c</sub>h<sub>, an</sub>d <sub>recor</sub>d th<sub>e s</sub>i<sub>gne</sub>d <sub>o</sub>f<sub>se</sub>t $\Delta \tau = \tau _ { P } - \tau$ i<sub>n</sub> <sub>secon</sub>d<sub>s.</sub> E<sub>ac</sub>h <sub>po</sub>i<sub>n</sub>t $p _ { k }$ i<sub>s</sub> t<sub>rans</sub>f<sub>orme</sub>d i<sub>n</sub>t<sub>o</sub> th<sub>e</sub> <sub>camera</sub> f<sub>rame</sub> throu<sub>g</sub>h the calibrated LiDAR-to-camera chain (com<sub>p</sub>osed via the odometry frame) and projected with the camera intrinsics K, <sub>y</sub>ielding image-plane coordinates $\pi _ { k }$ <sub>an</sub>d <sub>camera-</sub>f<sub>rame</sub> d<sub>ep</sub>th $d _ { k }$ <sub>.</sub> Th<sub>e</sub> <sub>suppor</sub>t <sub>se</sub>t <sub>o</sub>f i<sub>mage-</sub>l<sub>a</sub>t<sub>en</sub>t <sub>ce</sub>ll $q$ i<sub>s</sub>

$$
S _ { q } = \left\{ k : \pi _ { k } \in \mathrm { c e l l } q , d _ { k } > 0 \right\} , n _ { q } = | S _ { q } | .\tag{6}
$$

Projection performs no visibility reasoning: every in-frustum <sub>po</sub>i<sub>n</sub>t <sub>w</sub>ith <sub>pos</sub>iti<sub>ve</sub> d<sub>ep</sub>th <sub>con</sub>t<sub>r</sub>ib<sub>u</sub>t<sub>es</sub> t<sub>o</sub> it<sub>s</sub> <sub>ce</sub>ll<sub>,</sub> i<sub>nc</sub>l<sub>u</sub>di<sub>ng</sub> <sub>p</sub>oints from mutuall<sub>y</sub> occludin<sub>g</sub> surfaces (Section VII).

E<sub>xp</sub>li<sub>c</sub>it <sub>geo</sub>m<sub>e</sub>tr<sub>y s</sub>t<sub>a</sub>ti<sub>s</sub>ti<sub>cs.</sub> With th<sub>e mean ce</sub>ll d<sub>ep</sub>th $\bar { d } _ { q } =$ $\textstyle { \frac { 1 } { n _ { a } } } \sum _ { k \in S _ { q } } { \bar { d } } _ { k }$ <sub>,</sub> th<sub>e</sub> <sub>exp</sub>li<sub>c</sub>it <sub>componen</sub>t $g _ { q } \in \mathbb { R } ^ { 6 }$ <sub>o</sub>f <sub>an occup</sub>i<sub>e</sub>d <sub>ce</sub>ll $( n _ { q } > \dot { 0 } )$ i<sub>s</sub>

$$
\begin{array} { r } { g _ { q } = \big [ m _ { q } , \log ( 1 + \bar { d } _ { q } ) , \bar { d } _ { q } ^ { - 1 } , \log ( 1 + n _ { q } ) , \mathrm { V a r } _ { k \in { \cal S } _ { q } } ( d _ { k } ) , \Delta \tau \big ] , } \end{array}\tag{7}
$$

<sub>w</sub>h<sub>ere</sub> $m _ { q }$ is the validit<sub>y</sub> indicator, equal to 1 in occupied cells, <sub>an</sub>d th<sub>e var</sub>i<sub>ance</sub> i<sub>s</sub> t<sub>a</sub>k<sub>en over</sub> th<sub>e raw</sub> d<sub>ep</sub>th<sub>s</sub> $d _ { k }$ <sub>.</sub> Th<sub>e o</sub>f<sub>se</sub>t $\Delta \tau$ i<sub>s</sub> <sub>a</sub> <sub>per-v</sub>i<sub>ew</sub> <sub>sca</sub>l<sub>ar</sub> b<sub>roa</sub>d<sub>cas</sub>t i<sub>n</sub>t<sub>o</sub> <sub>every</sub> <sub>occup</sub>i<sub>e</sub>d <sub>ce</sub>ll<sub>.</sub> Em<sub>p</sub>t<sub>y ce</sub>ll<sub>s</sub> $( n _ { q } = 0 )$ <sup>carr</sup>y $g _ { q } = \mathbf { 0 }$

Learned <sub>p</sub>oint descri<sub>p</sub>tors and <sub>p</sub>acket<sub>.</sub> Th<sub>e</sub> l<sub>ea</sub>rn<sub>e</sub>d <sub>co</sub>m<sub>-</sub> <sub>ponen</sub>t <sub>averages</sub> th<sub>e</sub> f<sub>rozen</sub> Ut<sub>on</sub>i<sub>a</sub> d<sub>escr</sub>i<sub>p</sub>t<sub>ors</sub> <sub>o</sub>f th<sub>e</sub> <sub>suppor</sub>t<sub>-</sub> i<sub>ng po</sub>i<sub>n</sub>t<sub>s, an</sub>d th<sub>e pac</sub>k<sub>e</sub>t <sub>conca</sub>t<sub>ena</sub>t<sub>es</sub> b<sub>o</sub>th <sub>componen</sub>t<sub>s</sub>

$$
h _ { q } = \frac { 1 } { n _ { q } } \sum _ { k \in S _ { q } } { \mathrm { E } } _ { \mathrm { U t o n i a } } ( P ) _ { k } , \qquad \ell _ { q } = [ g _ { q } ; h _ { q } ] ,\tag{8}
$$

<sub>w</sub>h<sub>ere</sub> $\mathrm { E _ { U t o n i a } } ( P ) _ { k } \in \mathbb { R } ^ { C _ { u } }$ i<sub>s</sub> th<sub>e</sub> d<sub>escr</sub>i<sub>p</sub>t<sub>or</sub> <sub>o</sub>f <sub>po</sub>i<sub>n</sub>t $k ,$ so $\boldsymbol { h _ { q } } \in \mathbb { R } ^ { C _ { \iota } }$ <sub>.</sub> St<sub>ac</sub>ki<sub>ng</sub> $\ell _ { q }$ <sub>over</sub> th<sub>e</sub> $H \times W$ l<sub>a</sub>t<sub>en</sub>t <sub>gr</sub>id i<sub>e</sub>ld<sub>s</sub> th<sub>e</sub> <sub>p</sub>er-v<sup>i</sup>ew <sub>p</sub>ac<sup>k</sup>et $\ell \in \mathbb { R } ^ { ( \mathrm { \bar { 6 } } + C _ { u } ) \times H \times W }$ <sub>.</sub> C<sub>e</sub>ll<sub>s w</sub>ith $n _ { q } = 0$ are zero i<sub>n</sub> <sub>every</sub> <sub>c</sub>h<sub>anne</sub>l <sub>o</sub>f b<sub>o</sub>th <sub>componen</sub>t<sub>s.</sub>

(6)–(8) do not de<sub>p</sub>end on whether the view i<sub>s a source or a</sub> t<sub>arge</sub>t<sub>.</sub> Wh<sub>a</sub>t dif<sub>ers</sub> i<sub>s</sub> h<sub>ow</sub> th<sub>e pac</sub>k<sub>e</sub>t <sub>en</sub>t<sub>ers</sub> th<sub>e mo</sub>d<sub>e</sub>l<sub>.</sub> A <sub>source pac</sub>k<sub>e</sub>t i<sub>s m</sub>i<sub>xe</sub>d <sub>w</sub>ith th<sub>e v</sub>i<sub>ew</sub>’<sub>s</sub> i<sub>mage</sub> l<sub>a</sub>t<sub>en</sub>t<sub>, w</sub>h<sub>ereas a</sub> t<sub>arge</sub>t <sub>v</sub>i<sub>ew</sub> h<sub>as no</sub> i<sub>mage</sub> l<sub>a</sub>t<sub>en</sub>t<sub>, so</sub> it<sub>s pac</sub>k<sub>e</sub>t i<sub>s</sub> th<sub>e on</sub>l<sub>y o</sub>b<sub>serva</sub>ti<sub>on-</sub>d<sub>epen</sub>d<sub>en</sub>t <sub>con</sub>diti<sub>on</sub>i<sub>ng o</sub>f th<sub>a</sub>t <sub>v</sub>i<sub>ew</sub> (Section IV-D). ## E. Flow-Matching Training and Variable-Step Inference

Objective. M3GD retains GLD’s linear-path flow-matching objective with velocit<sub>y p</sub>rediction (Section III-B). The <sub>p</sub>rojected LiDAR packets modify only the conditioning variable $y ,$ so M3GD does not introduce a second prediction objective. S<sub>ec</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> VI<sub>-</sub>D2 t<sub>ra</sub>i<sub>ns a separa</sub>t<sub>e mo</sub>d<sub>e</sub>l <sub>on</sub> thi<sub>s a</sub>ll<sub>-zero</sub> i<sub>npu</sub>t f<sub>or every v</sub>i<sub>ew an</sub>d th<sub>ere</sub>b<sub>y measures</sub> th<sub>e no-</sub>LiDAR fl<sub>oor.</sub> At inference we apply classifier-free guidance with a jointly nulled <sub>con</sub>diti<sub>on</sub>

<sub>w</sub>h<sub>ere</sub> $\mathcal { D }$ <sub>nu</sub>ll<sub>s</sub> th<sub>e camera con</sub>diti<sub>on</sub>i<sub>ng an</sub>d <sub>a</sub>ll LiDAR <sub>pac</sub>k<sub>e</sub>t<sub>s</sub> <sub>s</sub>i<sub>mu</sub>lt<sub>aneous</sub>l<sub>y w</sub>hil<sub>e re</sub>t<sub>a</sub>i<sub>n</sub>i<sub>ng</sub> th<sub>e source</sub> i<sub>mage</sub> l<sub>a</sub>t<sub>en</sub>t<sub>s, an</sub>d $s { = } 1 . 5$

Inference. All V view latents are initialized from noise $z _ { 1 } \sim \mathcal { N } ( 0 , \mathbf { I } )$ and integrated jointly: a K-step Euler solver applies the guided velocit<sub>y</sub> of eq. (11) from t=1 to t=0 with ste<sub>p</sub> s<sup>i</sup>ze $1 / K$ <sub>.</sub> Th<sub>e source-v</sub>i<sub>ew</sub> l<sub>a</sub>t<sub>en</sub>t<sub>s are genera</sub>t<sub>e</sub>d <sub>a</sub>l<sub>ongs</sub>id<sub>e</sub> the targets and discarded. 2) Findings: DA3-Base level-0 is the most compatible feature level. Among the DA3-Base hierarch<sub>y</sub>, level-0 shows the strongest overall compatibility with projected Utonia features. It achieves the highest CKA and SVCCA-20, while ranking second on PCA channel correlation, Jaccard@10, and <sub>a</sub>fi<sub>n</sub>it<sub>y corre</sub>l<sub>a</sub>ti<sub>on.</sub> Alth<sub>oug</sub>h hi<sub>g</sub>h<sub>er</sub> l<sub>eve</sub>l<sub>s are compe</sub>titi<sub>ve on</sub> i<sub>so</sub>l<sub>a</sub>t<sub>e</sub>d l<sub>ow-</sub>di<sub>mens</sub>i<sub>ona</sub>l <sub>or ne</sub>i<sub>g</sub>hb<sub>or</sub>h<sub>oo</sub>d di<sub>agnos</sub>ti<sub>cs,</sub> th<sub>e</sub>i<sub>r</sub> <sub>raw-</sub>f<sub>ea</sub>t<sub>ure-space a</sub>li<sub>gnmen</sub>t i<sub>s wea</sub>k<sub>er, espec</sub>i<sub>a</sub>ll<sub>y</sub> i<sub>n</sub> CKA<sub>.</sub> This result is consistent with conditioning M3GD at level 0.
